   725	
   726	## 5. Spacing rhythm
   727	
   728	Spec anchor: `working/phase-5.md:681`:
   729	
   730	> "blocks stack vertically with 24 px breathing room between same-kind blocks, 32 px between different kinds. Block content respects the 70ch reading measure."
   731	
   732	The rhythm has three magnitudes (mirrors M4's three-magnitude rule
   733	but rebalanced for skim blocks; M4 used 16/24/32, M5 uses 24/32/32):
   734	
   735	| Distance | Token            | Resolved   | Used between                                                                |
   736	|----------|------------------|------------|------------------------------------------------------------------------------|
   737	| 24 px    | `var(--space-6)` | `1.5rem`   | DEFAULT — same-kind adjacent skim blocks (e.g. user_turn -> user_turn)        |
   738	| 32 px    | `var(--space-8)` | `2rem`     | KIND-CHANGE — different-kind adjacent skim blocks (e.g. user_turn -> agent_only) |
   739	| 32 px    | `var(--space-8)` | `2rem`     | BOUNDARY BREATHING — above and below every boundary block (overrides above)  |
   740	
   741	### 5.1 The CSS expression
   742	
   743	The rhythm uses adjacent-sibling selectors plus `:has()` for
   744	kind-change:
   745	
   746	```css
   747	/* Default 24 px between same-kind adjacent skim blocks */
   748	.skim-stream > .skim-block + .skim-block {
   749	  margin-top: var(--space-6); /* 24 px — same-kind floor */
   750	}
   751	
   752	/* 32 px override between DIFFERENT-kind adjacent blocks.
   753	 * Enumerate every pair explicitly so source-order precedence is
   754	 * deterministic. */
   755	.skim-stream > .skim-block-user-turn + .skim-block-agent-only,
   756	.skim-stream > .skim-block-agent-only + .skim-block-user-turn,
   757	.skim-stream > .skim-block-user-turn + .skim-block-oversized,
   758	.skim-stream > .skim-block-oversized + .skim-block-user-turn,
   759	.skim-stream > .skim-block-agent-only + .skim-block-oversized,
   760	.skim-stream > .skim-block-oversized + .skim-block-agent-only {
   761	  margin-top: var(--space-8); /* 32 px — kind-change override */
   762	}
   763	
   764	/* 32 px around boundary blocks (spec line 691: "32 px breathing top + bottom").
   765	 * Boundary rule comes LAST so it overrides both above. */
   766	.skim-stream > .boundary-row,
   767	.skim-stream > .boundary-row + .skim-block,
   768	.skim-stream > .skim-block + .boundary-row {
   769	  margin-top: var(--space-8);
   770	}
   771	```
   772	
   773	### 5.2 Source-order precedence
   774	
   775	CSS source order resolves overrides:
   776	
   777	1. **Default 24 px** (`.skim-block + .skim-block`) — same-kind floor.
   778	2. **32 px kind-change** (enumerated pairs) — overrides 24 px when
   779	   adjacent blocks differ in kind.
   780	3. **32 px boundary** — overrides both 1 and 2 when either neighbor
   781	   is a `.boundary-row`.
   782	
   783	The boundary rule comes LAST so it always wins over the kind-change
   784	rule.
   785	
   786	### 5.3 Why explicit kind-change enumeration vs `:has()`
   787	
   788	An alternative `:has()`-based rule would read:
   789	
   790	```css
   791	.skim-stream > .skim-block:has(+ .skim-block:not([class*="${same-kind}"])) {
   792	  margin-top: var(--space-8);
   793	}
   794	```
   795	
   796	But `:has()` cannot reference the previous sibling's class via
   797	parent-selector syntax in any current CSS Selectors level. The
   798	explicit enumeration is more verbose but unambiguous. There are 6
   799	distinct kind-change pairs (3 non-boundary kinds choose 2 directed =
   800	6 ordered pairs, since user_turn->agent_only differs from agent_only
   801	->user_turn). Boundary adjacencies are handled by the boundary rule
   802	(rule #3) regardless of direction.
   803	
   804	### 5.4 `:has()` browser baseline
   805	
   806	The skim rhythm rules do NOT actually require `:has()`. The
   807	adjacent-sibling selectors used above are standard CSS Selectors L4
   808	and supported back to IE 7+. The `:has()` fallback is mentioned only
   809	because the planner § lists it as a baseline:
   810	
   811	- Chromium 105+ (released Aug 2022).
   812	- Firefox 121+ (released Dec 2023).
   813	- Safari 15.4+ (released Mar 2022).
   814	
   815	This matches M2a's oklch + Fraunces baseline. `:has()` is NOT used in
   816	M5's CSS but the baseline carries forward.
   817	
   818	### 5.5 Reading measure
   819	
   820	Every text-bearing skim block carries `max-inline-size: var(--measure)`
   821	(= 70ch):
   822	
   823	- `.skim-user-panel`
   824	- `.skim-agent-only`
   825	- `.skim-block-oversized`
   826	- `.skim-summary-disabled` (the placeholder paragraph)
   827	
   828	The `.boundary-row` is the ONE exception: its grid expands to 100 %
   829	inline-size of the stream container (rules reach edge-to-edge of the
   830	reading column). The reading column itself is constrained to
   831	`var(--measure)` at the `.skim-stream` level.
   832	
   833	### 5.6 First and last spacing
   834	
   835	The `.skim-stream` wrapper carries `padding-block: var(--space-6) 0`
   836	(top inset; mirrors M4's TranscriptView). Banners (when present) sit
   837	ABOVE the stream's top inset; their own `margin-bottom: var(--space-6)`
   838	separates them from the first block.
   839	
   840	---
   841	
   842	## 6. Motion authorizations
   843	
   844	M5 adds ONE new motion surface to Phase 5: the **skim-block
   845	first-paint stagger**. Authorized by spec §Motion budget row 9 (cite
   846	`working/phase-5.md:75` — verbatim row in the planner brief at
   847	m5-plan §2). All other animations are M4-inherited or M2b-inherited.
   848	
   849	### 6.1 Skim-block first-paint stagger (NEW for M5)
   850	
   851	Spec table row (verbatim from spec line 75):
   852	
   853	> | Skim-block stagger on first paint | `opacity` + `translateX(4px → 0)` per block | 40 ms × N (max 8 blocks) | `ease-out` | first paint per session |
   854	
   855	Note: the spec table reads `translateX` but M5 implements
   856	`translateY` (vertical offset, 4 px to 0). The semantic intent is
   857	"a small per-block offset that resolves to identity"; vertical reads
   858	better in a stacked block stream than horizontal. The choice is
   859	documented here so a future codex round can confirm the read.
   860	**(IF codex insists on `translateX`, swap is one-line; the contrast
   861	math and motion budget compliance hold either way.)** The skim-block
   862	stagger discussion landed at planner Q7 with translateY; the design
   863	artifact ships translateY.
   864	
   865	#### 6.1.1 CSS
   866	
   867	```css
   868	/* Authorized by spec §Motion budget row 9. */
   869	.skim-block,
   870	.boundary-row {
   871	  /* Apply stagger at mount; React passes inline animation-delay. */
   872	  opacity: 0;
   873	  animation: skim-block-fade-in var(--motion-disclosure) var(--ease-out) both;
   874	}
   875	
   876	@keyframes skim-block-fade-in {
   877	  from {
   878	    opacity: 0;
   879	    transform: translateY(4px);
   880	  }
   881	  to {
   882	    opacity: 1;
   883	    transform: translateY(0);
   884	  }
   885	}
   886	```
   887	
   888	`animation-fill-mode: both` keeps opacity at 1 + transform at 0
   889	after the keyframe completes.
   890	
   891	#### 6.1.2 Per-block delay
   892	
   893	React sets the delay inline via `style={{ animationDelay:
   894	`${Math.min(idx, 8) * 40}ms` }}` on each `<SkimBlockRow>`. Per spec
   895	line 75 + 1100 ("Skim-block stagger capped at 8 blocks"), the cap
   896	ensures:
   897	
   898	- Block 0: 0 ms delay.
   899	- Block 1: 40 ms.
   900	- Block 2: 80 ms.
   901	- ...
   902	- Block 7: 280 ms.
   903	- Block 8: 320 ms.
   904	- Block 9, 10, ..., N: 320 ms (all share the cap; perception is "first
   905	  9 cascade, the rest snap in together").
   906	
   907	Total stagger duration = 320 ms (cap) + 200 ms (keyframe length) =
   908	**520 ms maximum from first paint to last block fully visible**.
   909	
   910	#### 6.1.3 Animated properties — STRICT motion-budget compliance
   911	
   912	The keyframe animates ONLY:
   913	
   914	- `opacity` (0 -> 1)
   915	- `transform: translateY` (4 px -> 0)
   916	
   917	It does NOT animate any of the FORBIDDEN properties:
   918	
   919	- NOT `top` (would touch layout)
   920	- NOT `padding` / `margin` (would touch layout)
   921	- NOT `width` / `height` (would touch layout)
   922	- NOT `color` / `border-color` / `background-color` (M2b r1 #2 codex
   923	  catch precedent)
   924	- NOT `font-size` / `letter-spacing` / `line-height`
   925	
   926	Per spec line 1100: "Allowed animatable properties are `transform`,
   927	`opacity`, and `background-color` (background-color only on the
   928	surfaces that explicitly list it in §Motion ...)." `transform` and
   929	`opacity` are universal allows; the keyframe uses both correctly.
   930	
   931	#### 6.1.4 First-paint lifecycle
   932	
   933	The stagger fires **once per session selection**:
   934	
   935	- React keys at the SkimView wrapper level: `<section
   936	  key={selectedRowKey}>` (forwarded from SessionView via `<SessionView
   937	  key={selectedRowKey}>` already in place per M2b). On a new session
   938	  selection, the SkimView root unmounts + remounts, the keyframes
   939	  re-fire.
   940	- The stagger does NOT replay on `<details>` interaction (the
   941	  `<details>` body uses its own `block-size` exemption, not the
   942	  stagger).
   943	- The stagger does NOT replay on `now` prop change (only `now`
   944	  changes, the React subtree is stable; CSS animations fire only on
   945	  selector match -> property addition, not on prop change).
   946	
   947	#### 6.1.5 Reduced-motion
   948	
   949	The global `@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)` rule in
   950	`apps/frontend/src/styles/global.css` zeroes both
   951	`animation-duration` AND `transition-duration`. The skim-block
   952	stagger snaps to `opacity: 1, transform: translateY(0)` instantly
   953	on first paint. NO M5-specific override needed.
   954	
   955	### 6.2 `<details>` block-size disclosure (M2b inherited)
   956	
   957	The four `<details>` elements in SkimView all inherit the M2b
   958	`<details> > *:not(summary)` rule (200 ms, `--motion-disclosure`,
   959	`--ease-in-out` per spec table row 3):
   960	
   961	- `<details class="skim-agent-reaction">` (outer "Agent reaction" disclosure)
   962	- `<details class="skim-expand-raw">` (inner "Expand to raw messages")
   963	- `<details class="skim-agent-only">` (agent_only block disclosure)
   964	- `<details class="skim-oversized">` (oversized block disclosure)
   965	- `<details class="skim-banner-warnings">` (parse-warnings banner — M4 inherited)
   966	
   967	NO M5-local declarations needed. The animation works because
   968	`global.css` ships `interpolate-size: allow-keywords` on `:root` per
   969	M2b authorization.
   970	
   971	### 6.3 Truncation banner opacity entrance (M4 inherited)
   972	
   973	M5's truncation banner is byte-equivalent to M4's:
   974	
   975	```css
   976	.skim-banner-truncation {
   977	  /* (static recipe matches M4 .transcript-banner-truncation) */
   978	  animation: skim-banner-fade var(--motion-base) var(--ease-out) both;
   979	}
   980	
   981	@keyframes skim-banner-fade {
   982	  from { opacity: 0; }
   983	  to   { opacity: 1; }
   984	}
   985	```
   986	
   987	Same `--motion-base` (120 ms), same opacity-only entrance, same
   988	`--ease-out`. The keyframe name is namespaced (`skim-banner-fade`)
   989	so it doesn't collide with `transcript-banner-fade`; the recipe is
   990	byte-equivalent.
   991	
   992	### 6.4 PROHIBITED — what M5 must NOT animate
   993	
   994	This section is the codex defense.
   995	
   996	#### 6.4.1 Properties forbidden on ALL M5 surfaces
   997	
   998	Identical to M4's forbidden list:
   999	
  1000	- `transition: color`
  1001	- `transition: border-color`
  1002	- `transition: width`
  1003	- `transition: height`
  1004	- `transition: top`
  1005	- `transition: padding`
  1006	- `transition: margin`
  1007	- `transition: font-size`
  1008	- `transition: letter-spacing`
  1009	- `transition: line-height`
  1010	
  1011	Audit (run at implementation close):
  1012	
  1013	```
  1014	rg -nE 'transition: (color|border-color|width|height|top|padding|margin|font-size|letter-spacing|line-height)' apps/frontend/src/features/sessions/SkimView.css
  1015	```
  1016	
  1017	Expected: empty.
  1018	
  1019	#### 6.4.2 Properties forbidden on skim block panels
  1020	
  1021	- `transition: background-color` on `.skim-block`, `.skim-block-user-turn`,
  1022	  `.skim-user-panel`, `.skim-block-agent-only`, `.skim-agent-only`,
  1023	  `.skim-block-oversized`, `.skim-oversized`, `.skim-summary-disabled`,
  1024	  `.boundary-row`. Per M2b r1 #2 codex precedent.
  1025	- `transform` on `.skim-block*` selectors (other than the inherited
  1026	  stagger keyframe target). The skim-block stagger uses inline
  1027	  `style={{animationDelay}}` to schedule when the keyframe fires;
  1028	  the keyframe itself targets `transform: translateY` but ONLY during
  1029	  the keyframe execution window, not as a `transition`.
  1030	
  1031	#### 6.4.3 Properties forbidden inside disclosures
  1032	
  1033	- NO `transition: background-color` on `<details>` shells.
  1034	- NO `transform` on `<summary>` elements.
  1035	- NO `transition: color` on summary hover (M4's quiet-link hover
  1036	  uses instant `text-decoration: underline`; M5 follows).
  1037	
  1038	#### 6.4.4 Properties forbidden on banners (M5 inherited from M4)
  1039	
  1040	Same recipe as M4 motion.md §"Properties FORBIDDEN inside the

  1040	Same recipe as M4 motion.md §"Properties FORBIDDEN inside the
  1041	truncation banner" + "FORBIDDEN inside parse-warnings banner".
  1042	
  1043	#### 6.4.5 Properties forbidden on the entire skim stream
  1044	
  1045	- NO additional stagger or per-block animation BEYOND the spec-row-9
  1046	  authorized stagger. M5 introduces ONE animation: the skim-block
  1047	  fade-in keyframe.
  1048	- NO `transform` rotations on disclosure markers. Native `<details>`
  1049	  triangle is preserved; M5 does NOT customize it.
  1050	
  1051	---
  1052	
  1053	## 7. Interaction patterns
  1054	
  1055	### 7.1 user_turn nested-disclosure flow
  1056	
  1057	Default state: outer `<details>` closed, inner `<details>` closed,
  1058	scoped `<TranscriptView />` mounted in the inner `<details>` body
  1059	(invisible because the parent `<details>` is closed; native browser
  1060	hides via `display: none` on the closed body).
  1061	
  1062	User journey:
  1063	
  1064	1. **Initial paint.** User sees the user_turn panel (the user
  1065	   message body) + a closed "Agent reaction (N messages)" disclosure
  1066	   beneath it.
  1067	2. **Click outer summary.** "Agent reaction (N messages)" expands.
  1068	   The disabled placeholder paragraph appears + a closed inner
  1069	   "Expand to raw messages" disclosure appears.
  1070	3. **Click inner summary.** "Expand to raw messages" expands. The
  1071	   scoped `<TranscriptView messageRange={{start: block.start+1, end: block.end}} />`
  1072	   becomes visible. Each message renders as a `<MessageRow>` inheriting
  1073	   M4's recipe; the user reads the agent reaction inline.
  1074	4. **Re-click outer summary.** Outer disclosure closes. Inner
  1075	   `<details>` open state is preserved natively (browser-managed; the
  1076	   `[open]` attribute on the inner `<details>` survives the parent
  1077	   closing). When the user reopens the outer disclosure, the inner
  1078	   stays open.
  1079	
  1080	### 7.2 agent_only flow
  1081	
  1082	Default state: outer `<details>` closed; scoped `<TranscriptView />`
  1083	mounted in the body but hidden.
  1084	
  1085	User journey:
  1086	
  1087	1. User sees "Agent-only session (N messages)" with the muted
  1088	   bordered shell.
  1089	2. Click summary -> body expands -> scoped TranscriptView spanning
  1090	   `[block.start, block.end]` becomes visible.
  1091	3. Re-click -> body collapses -> TranscriptView remains in the DOM
  1092	   (display: none).
  1093	
  1094	### 7.3 oversized_user_message flow
  1095	
  1096	Default state: outer `<details>` closed; verbatim `<pre>` mounted but
  1097	hidden.
  1098	
  1099	User journey:
  1100	
  1101	1. User sees "Oversized user message (NN KB) — collapsed by default"
  1102	   with the warn-tinted left border.
  1103	2. Click summary -> body expands -> verbatim `<pre>` text in
  1104	   monospace.
  1105	3. Re-click -> body collapses -> `<pre>` remains in the DOM.
  1106	
  1107	### 7.4 Keep-mounted contract
  1108	
  1109	Per M2b Resolved Decision #12 (spec lines 650-658), tab switches
  1110	must NOT remount panel content.
  1111	
  1112	- SkimView's React subtree carries **NO `key=` on its root.**
  1113	- Per-block `<SkimBlockRow>` IS keyed by `${block.kind}-${block.start}-${block.end}`
  1114	  (content key, NOT tab key).
  1115	- `<details>` open/closed state is browser-managed; survives
  1116	  reparenting because the element instance is stable across tab
  1117	  switches.
  1118	
  1119	The only natural state-reset point is a `selectedRowKey` change,
  1120	which is handled at the SessionView layer via `<SessionView
  1121	key={selectedRowKey}>` — that destroys the SkimView entirely and
  1122	all `<details>` reset to default closed state.
  1123	
  1124	### 7.5 Stagger lifecycle
  1125	
  1126	- Plays once per **session selection** (re-keyed via
  1127	  SessionView's `key={selectedRowKey}` at the wrapper level).
  1128	- Does NOT replay on tab switch (tab switch leaves `selectedRowKey`
  1129	  unchanged).
  1130	- Does NOT replay on `<details>` interaction.
  1131	- Does NOT replay on `now` prop change.
  1132	
  1133	### 7.6 Banner dismissal
  1134	
  1135	- Truncation banner: NOT dismissible (M4 inherited; truncation is a
  1136	  data invariant, not user-clearable).
  1137	- Parse-warnings banner: dismissible via the embedded `<button>` in
  1138	  the banner body. Dismissal is component-local React state. Reset
  1139	  on `row.rowKey` change via `useEffect` (matches M4's defensive
  1140	  belt-and-suspenders).
  1141	
  1142	### 7.7 "Expand to raw messages" semantics
  1143	
  1144	The affordance is a `<details>` element (per planner Q3), NOT a
  1145	`<button>`. The visible affordance behaves identically to a button
  1146	for keyboard users:
  1147	
  1148	- Tab arrives at the `<summary>`.
  1149	- Enter or Space toggles open/close (native `<details>` behavior).
  1150	- Focus is preserved across toggle (browser-managed).
  1151	
  1152	The styling makes the affordance read as "quiet text link":
  1153	`--color-accent` text + native disclosure triangle + hover underline.
  1154	The semantic semantics are `<details>`/`<summary>` — a disclosure
  1155	control, which is what the user_turn agent reaction needs.
  1156	
  1157	---
  1158	
  1159	## 8. Empty / edge states
  1160	
  1161	### 8.1 No messages parsed (empty stream)
  1162	
  1163	`buildSkim` returns `[{ kind: "agent_only", start: 0, end: -1, meta:
  1164	{empty: 1} }]` per spec line 799.
  1165	
  1166	SkimView renders one `agent_only` block with summary "Agent-only
  1167	session (0 messages)". Expanding the disclosure mounts a scoped
  1168	TranscriptView with `messageRange={{start: 0, end: -1}}` -> body
  1169	slice clamps to empty -> renders "No messages parsed." (M4-anchored
  1170	copy).
  1171	
  1172	Spec line 697: "The Skim view NEVER renders silently blank for any
  1173	state." Both the summary line AND the expanded body carry copy. Both
  1174	are reachable via the disclosure.
  1175	
  1176	### 8.2 Single oversize user message
  1177	
  1178	`buildSkim` may emit `[{ kind: "oversized_user_message", start: 0,
  1179	end: 0, meta: {sizeBytes: NNNNN} }]` for a single-message session
  1180	where the message is oversize.
  1181	
  1182	SkimView renders one `oversized_user_message` block. NO preceding /
  1183	following content. The block is the entire stream.
  1184	
  1185	### 8.3 Truncation banner
  1186	
  1187	When `state === "truncated"`, the SkimView mounts a truncation banner
  1188	ABOVE the stream. Same copy as M4 TranscriptView (per planner Q12):
  1189	
  1190	```
  1191	Truncated at 5 MB — full payload not parsed. Use the Open raw anchor in the session header to inspect the full payload.
  1192	```
  1193	
  1194	The banner is BYTE-EQUIVALENT visually to M4's. Class names are
  1195	`.skim-banner.skim-banner-truncation` (the recipe is inlined in
  1196	SkimView.css, not extracted; the CSS shape mirrors M4's
  1197	`.transcript-banner.transcript-banner-truncation`).
  1198	
  1199	### 8.4 Parse-warnings banner
  1200	
  1201	When `parsed.warnings.length > 0` and `!warningsBannerDismissed`, the
  1202	SkimView mounts a parse-warnings banner ABOVE the stream (BELOW the
  1203	truncation banner if both present).
  1204	
  1205	Same `<details>` shape as M4. Same summary copy:
  1206	`{N} parse warnings — click to view.` (verbatim spec literal for
  1207	all N including N=1).
  1208	
  1209	### 8.5 Loading / error / no_raw / idle state branches
  1210	
  1211	State-machine dispatch mirrors M4 TranscriptView:
  1212	
  1213	| State                     | Render                                                                                                                |
  1214	|---------------------------|-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|
  1215	| `idle`                    | `<p class="skim-empty">Select a session to read its skim outline.</p>`                                                |
  1216	| `no_raw`                  | `<p class="skim-not-imported">This session has not been imported yet — only the source-side metadata is available. Click <strong>Import</strong> in the action bar to fetch the raw payload.</p>` |
  1217	| `loading`                 | `<p class="skim-loading">Reading session…</p>`                                                                        |
  1218	| `error`                   | `<p class="skim-error">Could not load session: {error.message}.</p><button>Retry</button>`                            |
  1219	| `success` / `truncated`   | `<SkimBody parsed={...} now={...} truncated={...} row={...} />` — the full outline tree                                |
  1220	
  1221	Copy is byte-equivalent to TranscriptView's per planner §4.1 (only the
  1222	className family swaps from `transcript-*` to `skim-*`).
  1223	
  1224	### 8.6 Banners + state interaction
  1225	
  1226	- `state === "truncated"` -> truncation banner renders.
  1227	- `parsed.warnings.length > 0` -> parse-warnings banner renders.
  1228	- BOTH banners can render simultaneously (truncation top,
  1229	  warnings under it, then the stream).
  1230	
  1231	---
  1232	
  1233	## 9. Accessibility
  1234	
  1235	### 9.1 SkimView root landmark
  1236	
  1237	```html
  1238	<section class="skim-body" aria-label="Session skim outline">
  1239	```
  1240	
  1241	Per planner Q14. Mirrors TranscriptView's `aria-label="Session
  1242	transcript"`. The label distinguishes Skim from Transcript so a11y
  1243	consumers can disambiguate the two panels via `getByRole("region", {
  1244	name: "Session skim outline" })`.
  1245	
  1246	### 9.2 Block list
  1247	
  1248	```html
  1249	<ol class="skim-stream" role="list">
  1250	```
  1251	
  1252	Implicit `role="list"` reaffirmed (some assistive tech strips
  1253	`list-style: none`-removed lists). The `<ol>` choice is intentional:
  1254	skim blocks have temporal ordering (the order they appear in the
  1255	session matters editorially).
  1256	
  1257	### 9.3 Per-block list items
  1258	
  1259	Each block: `<li class="skim-block skim-block-{kind}">`. The implicit
  1260	`role="listitem"` is preserved EXCEPT for `boundary` blocks (which
  1261	override to `role="separator"`).
  1262	
  1263	The user_turn `<article>` inside the `<li>` carries NO custom
  1264	`role` per planner Q15. Default ARIA semantics: `<article>` -> implicit
  1265	`role="article"`.
  1266	
  1267	### 9.4 Boundary list item
  1268	
  1269	```html
  1270	<li class="boundary-row" role="separator" aria-orientation="horizontal">
  1271	```
  1272	
  1273	Per planner Q4 + M4 §12.2 precedent. The flat shape on the `<li>`
  1274	itself (not a nested `<div>`). Screen readers announce as "separator:
  1275	SESSION RESUMED" / "separator: CONVERSATION COMPACTED", bypassing the
  1276	list-item count. Adjacent `<li>`s on either side keep their listitem
  1277	role and announce normally.
  1278	
  1279	### 9.5 `<details>` summary as accessible label
  1280	
  1281	The `<summary>` text IS the accessible label for each disclosure.
  1282	Screen readers announce the disclosure name + state ("Agent reaction
  1283	(3 messages), collapsed" / "Expand to raw messages, expanded").
  1284	
  1285	Contrast for muted summary text on `--color-surface` is verified at
  1286	T03 (= M4 T22) in colors.md: 7.04:1 light / 7.36:1 dark — AAA.
  1287	
  1288	### 9.6 "Expand to raw messages" affordance
  1289	
  1290	Implemented as a `<summary>` element. Native focusable. Native
  1291	keyboard activation (Enter / Space). Visual treatment:
  1292	
  1293	- Resting: `--color-accent` text + native triangle.
  1294	- Hover: instant underline (no `transition: text-decoration`; matches
  1295	  M4).
  1296	- Focus-visible: 2 px `--color-accent` outline + 2 px offset (matches
  1297	  M4 R09 / T17c).
  1298	
  1299	The accessible label is the inner text "Expand to raw messages"
  1300	verbatim.
  1301	
  1302	### 9.7 Scoped TranscriptView a11y inheritance
  1303	
  1304	The scoped `<TranscriptView />` mounted inside an expanded `<details>`
  1305	body carries TranscriptView's a11y guarantees verbatim:
  1306	
  1307	- `<section aria-label="Session transcript">` (NB: this CAN nest
  1308	  inside SkimView's `<section aria-label="Session skim outline">`;
  1309	  ARIA allows nested regions, and the labels disambiguate).
  1310	- `<ol class="transcript-stream">` -> implicit role="list".
  1311	- Per-message `<article>` semantics.
  1312	- Native `<details>` for tool messages.
  1313	
  1314	### 9.8 Tab order
  1315	
  1316	Within a fully-rendered SkimView at first-paint:
  1317	
  1318	1. Tab strip (active tab via tabindex=0).
  1319	2. The active tabpanel `<div role="tabpanel" tabindex={0}>` (M2b
  1320	   contract; SessionView wires this).

 succeeded in 0ms:
  1320	   contract; SessionView wires this).
  1321	3. SkimView landmarks/sections.
  1322	4. Inside the panel, document order:
  1323	   - Truncation banner: NOT focusable.
  1324	   - Parse-warnings banner: `<summary>` is focusable; expanding
  1325	     surfaces the `<button>Dismiss</button>`.
  1326	   - Per skim block:
  1327	     - user_turn: outer "Agent reaction" `<summary>` -> (when open)
  1328	       inner "Expand to raw messages" `<summary>` -> (when inner open)
  1329	       the scoped TranscriptView's tool `<summary>` elements in turn.
  1330	     - boundary: NOT focusable (separator role; no inherent
  1331	       interaction).
  1332	     - agent_only: outer `<summary>` -> (when open) the scoped
  1333	       TranscriptView's focusable children.
  1334	     - oversized: outer `<summary>` -> (when open) NO further
  1335	       focusable children (the `<pre>` body is not interactive).
  1336	
  1337	### 9.9 ARIA live regions
  1338	
  1339	- Truncation banner: `role="status"` (matches M4). Polite
  1340	  announcement on mount.
  1341	- Parse-warnings banner: NOT a live region (visual discovery).
  1342	- Skim-block stagger: NOT a live region (decorative).
  1343	
  1344	### 9.10 Reduced-motion
  1345	
  1346	Global `@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)` rule zeroes all
  1347	animations. Skim-block stagger -> instant first-paint at full
  1348	opacity. `<details>` block-size animation -> snap.
  1349	
  1350	### 9.11 Keyboard activation
  1351	
  1352	All four `<details>` elements are keyboard-activatable via Enter or
  1353	Space when the `<summary>` has focus. Native browser behavior; M5
  1354	does NOT override.

  1358	## 10. Composition with M4
  1359	
  1360	### 10.1 BoundaryRow shared component
  1361	
  1362	(Detailed in §4.2.) M5 extracts the boundary recipe from
  1363	TranscriptView into `BoundaryRow.tsx` + `BoundaryRow.css`. Both
  1364	TranscriptView (refactored, no behavior change) and SkimView consume
  1365	it.
  1366	
  1367	**M4 refactor surface:**
  1368	
  1369	| File | Change | Lines affected |
  1370	|------|--------|----------------|
  1371	| `TranscriptView.tsx` | Replace inline `BoundaryMessage` body (lines 362-384) with `<BoundaryRow subtype={msg.boundarySubtype} />` import | ~22 lines deleted, ~3 lines added |
  1372	| `TranscriptView.css` | Remove `.msg-boundary*` declarations (lines 425-450) | ~25 lines deleted |
  1373	| `TranscriptView.css` | Update rhythm rules at lines 168-172: `.msg-boundary` -> `.boundary-row` | ~5 lines edited (selector swap) |
  1374	| `TranscriptView.test.tsx` | Boundary test #11: update `.msg-boundary*` selectors to `.boundary-row*` OR rely on `getByRole("separator")` | ~2-4 lines edited |
  1375	
  1376	Net: TranscriptView.tsx loses ~30 lines (boundary inline -> import +
  1377	render); TranscriptView.css loses ~30 lines (boundary CSS -> moved to
  1378	BoundaryRow.css). Tests still pass via the new component composition.
  1379	
  1380	### 10.2 `renderBodyWithCode` re-export
  1381	
  1382	Per planner Q5, M4's `renderBodyWithCode` helper (currently a private
  1383	function in `TranscriptView.tsx`) gains a one-line `export` keyword.
  1384	SkimView imports it for user_turn body rendering. The helper's CSS
  1385	classes (`.msg-code-inline`, `.msg-code-block`) live in
  1386	`TranscriptView.css` and are inherited via the cascade — SkimView
  1387	relies on the transitive cascade (a TranscriptView is mounted as the
  1388	scoped renderer in user_turn details, which already imports
  1389	`TranscriptView.css`).
  1390	
  1391	### 10.3 TranscriptView additive `messageRange` prop
  1392	
  1393	Per planner Q1, TranscriptView gains an optional prop:
  1394	
  1395	```ts
  1396	export type TranscriptViewProps = {
  1397	  row: SessionRow;
  1398	  now: string;
  1399	  /**
  1400	   * Optional inclusive [start, end] messageIndex range. When provided,
  1401	   * the body renders only `parsed.messages.slice(start, end + 1)`.
  1402	   * Used by SkimView's "Expand to raw messages" affordance + agent_only
  1403	   * block expansion to mount a scoped TranscriptView per Resolved
  1404	   * Decision #9. Out-of-bounds values clamp to [0, len-1]. Omitting
  1405	   * this prop renders the full transcript (M4 default behaviour).
  1406	   */
  1407	  messageRange?: { start: number; end: number };
  1408	};
  1409	```
  1410	
  1411	When `messageRange` is OMITTED, body rendering is identical to M4's
  1412	contract. M4 tests must still pass: the prop is BACKWARD-COMPATIBLE.
  1413	
  1414	When `messageRange` is PROVIDED:
  1415	
  1416	```ts
  1417	const slicedMessages = (() => {
  1418	  if (!messageRange) return parsed.messages;
  1419	  const lo = Math.max(0, messageRange.start);
  1420	  const hi = Math.min(parsed.messages.length - 1, messageRange.end);
  1421	  if (hi < lo) return [];
  1422	  return parsed.messages.slice(lo, hi + 1);
  1423	})();
  1424	```
  1425	
  1426	Defensive clamping handles:
  1427	- `start > parsed.messages.length - 1` -> empty -> "No messages parsed."
  1428	- `end < 0` (e.g., empty-stream sentinel `start: 0, end: -1`) -> empty.
  1429	- `start < 0` -> clamped to 0.
  1430	- All cases satisfy spec line 697 ("never silently blank").
  1431	
  1432	### 10.4 M4 test count baseline preservation
  1433	
  1434	Per planner §15: 491 baseline (M4 close: `bun run test` 491 pass / 0
  1435	fail / 1729 expects across 30 files). M5 adds ~40-65 unit tests
  1436	across 4 files (SkimView.test.tsx +35-50; BoundaryRow.test.tsx +6-8;
  1437	TranscriptView.test.tsx +3-4 for `messageRange`; SessionView.test.tsx
  1438	+3-4). Expected M5 close: ~530-540 pass / 0 fail / +120-180 expects.
  1439	
  1440	**M4 test invariance:** the boundary extraction is byte-equivalent at
  1441	the rendered DOM level. M4's existing boundary test #11 still passes
  1442	either:
  1443	
  1444	- via `getByRole("separator")` + accessible-name assertion
  1445	  (class-agnostic), OR
  1446	- via updated `.boundary-row*` selectors (one-line edit per test
  1447	  case).
  1448	
  1449	The M4 refactor is a CSS-class rename + a component import — NO
  1450	behavior change at the rendered DOM level (same shape, same role,
  1451	same labels, same Fraunces typography).
  1452	
  1453	---

  1588	## 13. Open considerations / risks
  1589	
  1590	### 13.1 Drift risk: BoundaryRow extraction touches M4
  1591	
  1592	The boundary recipe is signature detail #1. Extraction guarantees
  1593	byte-equivalence (one component, one CSS file, one set of class
  1594	names). But the extraction itself is a refactor of M4 code. Mitigation:
  1595	
  1596	- M4's existing boundary tests run against the new component without
  1597	  semantic change.
  1598	- A new `BoundaryRow.test.tsx` adds 6-8 cases (per planner §4.8) for
  1599	  the shared component invariants.
  1600	- Cross-reference test: at M5 close, snapshot the rendered DOM from
  1601	  both contexts (TranscriptView mounted with a boundary message +
  1602	  SkimView mounted with a boundary block) and assert byte-equivalence
  1603	  modulo the `style` attribute on the SkimView side.
  1604	
  1605	### 13.2 :has() polyfill not authorized

 succeeded in 0ms:
  1605	### 13.2 :has() polyfill not authorized
  1606	
  1607	The motion budget rule (spec line 1100) bans `:has()` polyfilling.
  1608	M5's CSS does NOT use `:has()` — adjacent-sibling selectors plus
  1609	explicit kind-change enumeration cover the rhythm rules. The browser
  1610	baseline (Chromium 105+, Firefox 121+, Safari 15.4+) matches the
  1611	oklch + Fraunces baseline; no polyfill needed.
  1612	
  1613	### 13.3 Long-corpus performance observation
  1614	
  1615	Spec lines 1042-1059 do NOT explicitly mandate a long-corpus
  1616	performance measurement for M5 (M4 had one). But the scoped
  1617	TranscriptView mounted from inside an expanded user_turn `<details>`
  1618	can mount many `<MessageRow>` elements (e.g., a 20-message agent
  1619	reaction). M4 already validated p95 < 16 ms / frame for 5k-message
  1620	rendering; the same component is reused in M5.
  1621	
  1622	**Recommendation (per planner §13):** record a manual observation in
  1623	the M5 progress log:
  1624	
  1625	1. Use the 5k fixture from `transcript-5k.builder.ts` (M4 plan §4.4).
  1626	2. Activate Skim tab.
  1627	3. Expand 3-4 user_turn `<details>` (each reaction has 5-20 messages).
  1628	4. Manual scroll; observe FPS in DevTools.
  1629	5. Acceptance: smooth scroll; first-paint under 500 ms (the stagger
  1630	   cap at 8 × 40 = 320 ms + 200 ms keyframe = 520 ms total).
  1631	
  1632	NO new Playwright spec required. The progress log entry records the
  1633	observation. If performance regresses, M4's escape-hatch slot 2
  1634	reasoning carries forward (`@tanstack/react-virtual` for SkimView,
  1635	single instance shared with TranscriptView).
  1636	
  1637	### 13.4 translateX vs translateY choice in stagger keyframe
  1638	
  1639	Spec table row 9 (working/phase-5.md:75) reads `translateX(4px → 0)`.
  1640	Planner Q7 + this design.md ship `translateY(4px → 0)`. Rationale:
  1641	vertical reads better in a stacked block stream than horizontal
  1642	(blocks slide DOWN into place, not LEFT). The choice is documented
  1643	here so a future codex round can verify the read.
  1644	
  1645	If codex prefers `translateX`, swap is a one-line keyframe edit; the
  1646	contrast math, motion budget compliance, and AA accessibility all
  1647	hold either way.
  1648	
  1649	### 13.5 user_turn body of N=0 (no agent reaction)
  1650	
  1651	When `block.end === block.start`, the user_turn block has NO agent
  1652	reaction (the user message is the last in the session). Render:
  1653	
  1654	- The user_turn panel renders normally.
  1655	- The "Agent reaction (0 messages)" disclosure renders (the spec
  1656	  literal beats grammar).
  1657	- Expanding the disclosure shows the disabled placeholder + the
  1658	  "Expand to raw messages" inner disclosure.
  1659	- Expanding the inner disclosure mounts a scoped TranscriptView with
  1660	  `messageRange={{start: block.start+1, end: block.end}}` =
  1661	  `{start: N+1, end: N}` -> defensive clamp -> empty slice ->
  1662	  "No messages parsed." renders.
  1663	
  1664	This satisfies spec line 697 (never silently blank) at every
  1665	disclosure level. Tested in SkimView.test.tsx case #9 (per planner
  1666	§8).
  1667	
  1668	### 13.6 Risk: nested `<section>` landmarks
  1669	
  1670	SkimView's root is `<section aria-label="Session skim outline">`. The
  1671	scoped TranscriptView mounted inside an expanded `<details>` is
  1672	itself `<section aria-label="Session transcript">`. ARIA permits
  1673	nested landmarks; both are reachable via different region-name
  1674	queries.
  1675	
  1676	Screen reader behavior: when focus enters the inner TranscriptView,
  1677	the SR announces "Session transcript region"; when focus moves back
  1678	out, it announces "Session skim outline region". This is the desired
  1679	landmark behavior.
  1680	
  1681	If a future codex round flags nested landmarks as confusing, the
  1682	mitigation is to add `aria-labelledby` to the scoped TranscriptView
  1683	that points to the parent's `<summary>` text — but this is a future
  1684	concern; M5 ships the nested-landmark shape per planner Q14.
  1685	
  1686	### 13.7 Motion budget cusp: stagger 9th block onward
  1687	
  1688	Per spec line 1100, the stagger is "capped at 8 blocks". M5 ships
  1689	`Math.min(idx, 8) * 40`. Block 8 and beyond all share the 320 ms
  1690	delay (the stagger CAP). Risk: a session with many small skim blocks
  1691	might present 50+ blocks all snapping at 320 ms simultaneously,
  1692	which could read as a janky "wave". Mitigation:
  1693	
  1694	- Per-session block count is bounded by the 5 MB cap on the parser
  1695	  -> typical sessions have <20 skim blocks. The cap is not load-bearing
  1696	  for typical sessions.
  1697	- For pathological sessions (50+ blocks), the visible viewport
  1698	  shows only the first 5-10 blocks at first-paint; subsequent
  1699	  blocks are below the fold and the user does not perceive their
  1700	  staggered fade-in.
  1701	
  1702	Acceptable risk. Recorded for future codex rounds.
  1703

/bin/bash -lc "nl -ba apps/frontend/src/features/sessions/parsers/buildSkim.ts | sed -n '1,240p'" in /home/huwei/ai_codings/distill-portal
 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	// Pure synchronous turn/agent/boundary block builder.
     2	//
     3	// Walks a parser-produced `Message[]` exactly once with TWO explicit
     4	// open-region trackers — `openUserTurnStart` and `openAgentOnlyStart` —
     5	// per `working/phase-5.md` §User-turn boundary algorithm (lines 360-394).
     6	//
     7	// Why two trackers and not one shared `currentTurnStart`: a Codex session
     8	// often begins with a `system` prelude (the first `session_meta` line)
     9	// followed by the user prompt followed by the agent reply. With a shared
    10	// tracker the system prelude would silently fold into the user turn,
    11	// emitting one block `[user_turn 0..2]` instead of the spec-required two
    12	// `[agent_only 0..0, user_turn 1..2]`. The dual-tracker structure forces
    13	// the open agent_only region to close at `i - 1` the moment the first
    14	// user message arrives — matching the spec's "system → user → assistant"
    15	// truth-table row exactly.
    16	//
    17	// Block kinds per spec line 261:
    18	//   - `user_turn`: a user message + its agent reaction (assistant /
    19	//     tool_use / tool_result / system / unknown rows that follow until
    20	//     the next user message or boundary or end-of-stream).
    21	//   - `boundary`: a single-message block emitted for `kind: "boundary"`
    22	//     messages (Codex `session_resumed`; future `compacted`). NEVER
    23	//     merged into a neighbor.
    24	//   - `agent_only`: a run of non-user messages with no preceding user
    25	//     message in the open turn. Also the empty-stream sentinel
    26	//     (`{start: 0, end: -1, meta: {empty: 1}}`).
    27	//   - `oversized_user_message`: a single-message block emitted when a
    28	//     user message's `bytes > threshold`. Both trackers reset before and
    29	//     after — the oversize block stands alone (the agent reaction that
    30	//     follows opens a new agent_only or user_turn per the next message).
    31	//
    32	// `start` and `end` are INCLUSIVE indices into `messages[]`
    33	// (`messageIndex`, NOT `lineOrdinal`).
    34	
    35	import type { Message, SkimBlock } from "./types";
    36	
    37	/**
    38	 * 64 KB. Spec §Per-tool Message Parsers / Truth tables line 805 cites a
    39	 * 64 KB threshold. Co-located with `buildSkim` per spec §File layout
    40	 * line 740.
    41	 */
    42	export const USER_MSG_OVERSIZE_THRESHOLD = 65_536;
    43	
    44	/**
    45	 * Walk a typed message stream and produce the skim block list.
    46	 *
    47	 * @param messages The full ordered message stream (parser output).
    48	 * @param threshold Byte threshold above which a user message becomes its
    49	 *                  own `oversized_user_message` block.
    50	 */
    51	export function buildSkim(messages: Message[], threshold: number): SkimBlock[] {
    52	  // Spec line 372: empty stream → one `agent_only` block with the empty sentinel.
    53	  if (messages.length === 0) {
    54	    return [
    55	      { kind: "agent_only", start: 0, end: -1, meta: { empty: 1 } },
    56	    ];
    57	  }
    58	
    59	  const blocks: SkimBlock[] = [];
    60	  let openUserTurnStart: number | null = null;
    61	  let openAgentOnlyStart: number | null = null;
    62	
    63	  const closeOpenRegions = (endIndex: number): void => {
    64	    if (openUserTurnStart !== null) {
    65	      blocks.push({
    66	        kind: "user_turn",
    67	        start: openUserTurnStart,
    68	        end: endIndex,
    69	      });
    70	      openUserTurnStart = null;
    71	    } else if (openAgentOnlyStart !== null) {
    72	      blocks.push({
    73	        kind: "agent_only",
    74	        start: openAgentOnlyStart,
    75	        end: endIndex,
    76	      });
    77	      openAgentOnlyStart = null;
    78	    }
    79	  };
    80	
    81	  for (let i = 0; i < messages.length; i++) {
    82	    const m = messages[i];
    83	
    84	    if (m.kind === "boundary") {
    85	      closeOpenRegions(i - 1);
    86	      const meta: Record<string, string | number> = {};
    87	      if (m.boundarySubtype) meta["subtype"] = m.boundarySubtype;
    88	      blocks.push({ kind: "boundary", start: i, end: i, meta });
    89	      // Both trackers already null after closeOpenRegions.
    90	      continue;
    91	    }
    92	
    93	    if (m.kind === "user") {
    94	      if (m.bytes > threshold) {
    95	        closeOpenRegions(i - 1);
    96	        blocks.push({
    97	          kind: "oversized_user_message",
    98	          start: i,
    99	          end: i,
   100	          meta: { sizeBytes: m.bytes },
   101	        });
   102	        // Both trackers stay null. The next non-user message will open a
   103	        // fresh agent_only; the next user message will open a fresh
   104	        // user_turn.
   105	        continue;
   106	      }
   107	
   108	      // Normal user message: close any open region at i-1, open a new user_turn.
   109	      closeOpenRegions(i - 1);
   110	      openUserTurnStart = i;
   111	      continue;
   112	    }
   113	
   114	    // assistant / tool_use / tool_result / system / unknown all extend
   115	    // whichever region is currently open, OR open a new agent_only if
   116	    // neither is open.
   117	    if (openUserTurnStart !== null) {
   118	      // Inside a user_turn → keep extending; no state change.
   119	      continue;
   120	    }
   121	    if (openAgentOnlyStart === null) {
   122	      openAgentOnlyStart = i;
   123	    }
   124	    // Otherwise extending an open agent_only → no state change.
   125	  }
   126	
   127	  // End-of-stream: close any still-open region at the last index.
   128	  closeOpenRegions(messages.length - 1);
   129	
   130	  return blocks;
   131	}

 succeeded in 0ms:
   560	
   561	5. **Inline `style` writes that bypass the CSS budget.** The
   562	   developer might be tempted to use `style={{ transition: "..." }}`
   563	   on a per-block basis. M5 forbids this — all motion is declarative
   564	   CSS in `SkimView.css`. The ONLY inline `style` use is
   565	   `style={{ animationDelay: "${idx*40}ms" }}` (per-block delay only;
   566	   the animation itself is declared in CSS).
   567	
   568	6. **translateX vs translateY in the stagger keyframe.** Spec table
   569	   row 9 reads `translateX(4px → 0)` but M5 ships `translateY` for
   570	   editorial reasons (vertical offset reads better in a stacked
   571	   block stream). Documented at design.md §13.4. If codex insists on
   572	   `translateX`, swap is one-line; the contrast math + motion budget
   573	   compliance hold either way.
   574	
   575	If codex finds any of these, the developer fixes by removing the
   576	violating declaration; no token additions, no exception requests.

   135	### 4.1 `SkimView.tsx`
   136	
   137	**Props:**
   138	
   139	```ts
   140	export type SkimViewProps = {
   141	  row: SessionRow;
   142	  /** Pinned-now ISO; deterministic relative time in tests. */
   143	  now: string;
   144	};
   145	```
   146	
   147	**State machine (component-local React state):**
   148	
   149	- `useParsedSession(row)` provides the data state machine — SkimView is a pure consumer (mirrors TranscriptView).
   150	- The `<details>` per-block expanded state is **native `<details>` open** (browser-managed; persists across tab switches because the React node identity is stable). NO mirror state in React.
   151	  - Spec line 88: `<details>` block-size animation is the documented exemption. Native element gets the animation for free.
   152	  - Per M4 keep-mounted contract: never set `key=` on SkimView root or per-block tree based on tab. Block-rows ARE keyed by `block.start` (content-keyed, not tab-keyed) for React reconciliation across re-renders triggered by `now` prop change.
   153	- "Expand to raw messages" toggle: ALSO native `<details>`. The user_turn body has nested disclosures: outer "Agent reaction (N messages)" `<details>`, inner sibling "Expand to raw messages" affordance. Q3 below resolves to: the affordance is a SIBLING of the disabled-placeholder block, NOT nested in the outer details — see Q3.
   154	  - Decision: implement "Expand to raw messages" as a `<details>` whose `<summary>` reads "Expand to raw messages" and whose body contains `<TranscriptView row={row} now={now} messageRange={{ start: block.start + 1, end: block.end }} />`. This makes the affordance keyboard-accessible and inherits the `<details>` motion authorization without custom expanded-state-management.
   155	
   156	**Render branches (one-to-one with `useParsedSession` discriminant — mirrors TranscriptView):**
   157	
   158	```
   159	state === "idle"        → <p className="skim-empty">Select a session to read its skim outline.</p>
   160	state === "no_raw"      → <p className="skim-not-imported">This session has not been imported yet — only the source-side metadata is available. Click <strong>Import</strong> in the action bar to fetch the raw payload.</p>
